Game Time [Merge] (1)

"Oh dear...... So boring...... You lie on the sofa complaining, swinging your legs casually, then bury your head in the sofa.

Of course—no one cares about you, or rather, the Dark People don't care about you. After all, you know what kind of person Anton Tenna is in this world. If she allowed you to stay here and cause trouble, that's more or less. Although you don't even know why he allowed you to stay here, it's probably because the Dark Folk are naturally caring for the Light Ones.

You didn't care about any of that—since no one answered you, you simply looked up, turned over, and sat back down on the sofa. Then you immediately saw Tenna sitting on the leather chair on the other side, the screen pitch black, as if she hadn't noticed what you were doing at all.

You couldn't help but feel speechless, walking over with your hands on your hips and looking down at him—well, that's not possible, since he's only about as tall as you when he's sitting down. In fact, you need to lift your head slightly to see him clearly.

You stand on tiptoe, a bit annoyed, trying to grab his antenna, but the next second his screen lights up, and the two antennas shake and slip away from your hand.

He looks at you expressionlessly: "What do you want to do?" ”

You saw his expression shrink slightly. Whether you said it or not, you were still a little afraid of him—after all, you knew he wasn't easy to talk to, but you still straightened your back and stared straight at his screen—after all, you couldn't see his eyes.

"Hmm, ahem, because it's too boring, I've decided to requisition your TV screen for gaming! After all, besides your TV, there's no other TV in your lounge that can be used." You put your hands on your hips and announced, even though you didn't know why, despite the room being meant for rest and entertainment, there was nothing else except the sofa—oh, and there's this one you instinctively found a bit annoying.

"What game?" His voice was cold and indifferent, and you couldn't help but take two steps back—after all, you started to regret coming to him directly. If you'd known, you should have looked for a TV somewhere else first. Anyway, there must be other TVs with big screens in this world, so why face such a ...... A guy you can't even quite put your finger on.

You pull out those few game CDs on you, hmm...... A fighting game, a two-player game, no need for this, you can't find anyone to play with you. A two-player survival game...... It's probably a drifting adventure on the sea, kind of like Life of Pi...... No, single-player is a bit too difficult, you can't play it. Two-player cooperative puzzle game...... Actually, you're quite tempted, but you sneak a glance at the guy in front of you and finally snort coldly in your heart, not asking if he wants to play games with you! He's really annoying; who knows what he'd say if we played together......

In the end, you chose a single-player adventure game—a simple Japanese RPG, a classic Dragon Quest story. After searching for a long time, you finally found a game disc you thought was quite interesting, then raised it high, your eyes sparkling: "This one!" "I want to play this!" Here, here you go. "You hand it to Tenna, looking at him expectantly.

Actually, Tenna had already noticed your sneaky glance earlier—he really wanted to say something more, but held back. After all, he knew that what he said was probably something you didn't want to hear, and for now, he didn't want you to leave here angry.

Tenna took the disc. I don't know exactly how he operated it, but he handed you a game console, then sat down opposite you on the sofa. He held the TV head and started projecting the game screen to you, while you happily sat cross-legged on the sofa and pressed buttons to start your adventure story.

Although there is a lot of textual storyline, this game still tests puzzle-solving thinking and skill, since it's somewhat of a 2D platforming puzzle-solving adventure game, and there are quite a few small monsters to clear along the way.

You seriously solve the puzzles along the way; those levels are a piece of cake for you—but in terms of combat controls, it's unclear whether your reflexes are slow or ...... The combat system in this game isn't very well designed. After all, you don't feel any impact at all, and the controls feel a bit sluggish. You even suspect it's a bit of a transmission issue, since pressing a button usually delays a little before the character actually acts.

Once again, the character dies. After the familiar GameOver CG, you purse your lips but don't lose heart. Instead, you regroup and decide to do it again. After so many times, you have to remember the boss's move moves and what you should do next.

But—while you were focused on attacking the boss, you heard Tenna's voice, casual and tinged with mockery: "...... Looks like you're bad at fighting games, no real skill. ”

He just watched you die several times, and even he knows how to get through it.

You choked for a moment, distracted by what he said, your hand instinctively trembled. The character who could have perfectly dodged the boss's finishing blow and landed the final blow shifted, landing in an inaccurate spot, and was killed by the boss's strafing fire. The gameover CG reappeared on the screen.

You angrily slammed the controller onto the sofa: "Yeah, I know I'm terrible. I've never been good at combat—but, but I was about to clear it just now, and it was you who suddenly said something that distracted me!" You started to blame him. After all, you were very self-aware, and you knew you were bad at playing. But as long as you enjoyed the game, that was enough. You didn't mind trying a few more times to clear it.

But you were originally quite interested, but after hearing that one sentence, your enthusiasm suddenly faded. Tenna just snorted coldly: "I've been watching all along, and your gaming skills really aren't great." ”

You glared at Tenna with red eyes—well, you don't even know if he actually saw it. You just raised your voice and said, "Bad at it, that's bad! If I try a few more times, I'll clear it. If you keep talking......"

After saying that, you still press the confirm button and decide to restart and challenge the boss again. But for some reason, after this restart, the screen actually starts to garbage, black-and-white noise appears on the TV screen, and even—intermittently and suddenly goes black. You try to control your character forward, triggering the boss again, but this time, because of the garbled screen and black screen, the character dies quickly.

Accompanied by the sound effect of death, Tenna's slightly mocking voice said, "I thought after so many times, you would remember it completely." ”

“…… The boss's jumping and moving positions are different! Even if I remember his attack order, I can't always guess where he moves! "You said angrily, feeling like your rage was almost full, but you still held back and started another round. This time, the screen was completely guttered. You didn't know if he was deliberately going against you. If he hadn't finished this boss and cleared the game, your intuition would have been that he wouldn't hand over the game disc so easily. You'd have already run the entire dark world looking for other TVs."

You puff out your cheeks, holding your breath as you control your character forward. Indeed, you've fought many times, so you have memorized the controls and map—you cleared that boss with a garbled screen and no visible scenario.

Tenna also raised her voice, speaking in a slightly surprised tone: "Oh? Isn't this something you can do?" ”

“…… Yeah, but if I hadn't died so many times before, how could I have done it! "You dropped the game console. Anyway, you've cleared this game, and you don't plan to play anymore. Besides, you just wanted to pass the time, but now you've been holding in all your anger, so you're completely fed up and not bored at all.

You feel like you're about to cry from anger—but in the end, you just step forward and pound the TV's head hard to vent your anger, your eyes red. You don't want to cry in front of him anymore, but you still have some shame: "Broken TV!! I'm not playing with you anymore, I'll go find another TV to use. I refuse to believe there are no other televisions in this TV world!" ”

You looked at the decorative screen in front of you, seemingly getting even more imposing, and you didn't care what else he would say—in short, it was probably something unpleasant. Anyway, you rushed out the door—yes, you pulled down the lounge door too, and you wanted Tenna to sit there and enjoy the cold wind!

But after running a short distance, you stop, panting, sniff twice, wipe the tears streaming down your eyes, and sob twice. You feel wronged, but you also know that if you really tell Tenna, at most he'll just apologize stiffly. But—but you don't want that. If things end up unpleasant, why not just do it well from the start? If you end up going our separate ways, then not meeting at first is probably the best choice—at least that's what you think.

You don't want a rigid apology, you don't expect that kind of apology—but if he comes after you to apologize, it's not that you can't forgive him for now.

You just ...... You just want to give him another chance. Even though you always tell yourself that this is the last time, there's nothing you can do—who told you not to really be ruthless with him? Who told you to ...... to like this guy? You don't even know why you like him; maybe this is just the origin of unpredictable love.

So your steps slowed down more and more, curling up in a corner and quietly crying. You felt a bit awkward, hoping Tenna would catch up and find you, but also wishing he never came back, so you could tell yourself that it really was time to give up.

Until you sneak around this corner, sneaking around and spotting a deep blue figure.

TBC.

You sniffled, feeling even more wronged, wanting to cry your heart out without caring why you were acting recklessly—fine, fine, you don't even know if crying would help, but your eyes rolled around, then you sobbed loudly, wiped the corners of your eyes, and sobbed loudly, choking and sobbing, crying until you could barely breathe. Just like every time before.

And just like every other time, the TV head turned around and walked toward you after hearing the sound. His screen was pitch black, and no one understood what he was really feeling about. And you didn't care, since he made you cry because of it, why care about all that?

Tenna squatted down in front of you, your face covered in tears and cheeks flushed from crying. His screen was still pitch black, but you knew very well that this was probably the biggest concession he could make right now. You sniffed, quieted down a bit, but still sobbing, then reached out and grabbed his antenna—avoiding the spot where the wound had been before, then shaking it wildly.

"I hate you...... I hate you the most, I hate you the most! Big villain! "You let out your emotions recklessly, uttering completely sarcastic remarks. Yes, you have tried to stop liking him...... But you can't do it. Maybe, in love, the first person to fall in love is always the one who gives in? You don't know.

Tenna just lowered the TV head a little, letting you hold the antenna and shake it. He felt his mind shaken, and as the antenna shook, the sea inside also wavered. He saw your sadness and your affection. He knows...... You love him.

So he wants to keep you, probably just because of a simple, simple wish. He doesn't know if he loves you; he just can't bear to give up, but he's unwilling to face these things—he's always been aware. He just feels he can't do that—he can't love you. After all, if you're just deceiving him, and leaving after everything is over, then what will Anton Tenna do?

So at this moment, he just lowered his head, letting you hold the antenna, casually, a bit coldly, and a bit stiffly saying, "Mm." ”

Then he tugged your hips and picked you up. You curled up in his arms, and he turned to walk back toward the lounge.

The pitch-black screen finally lit up again, but the corners of his mouth were still curved. He touched the tip of his nose, not knowing why.

You were stunned for two seconds, then naturally let go. Then you snapped back to reality, blushing, slapping Tenna's screen and shouting, "You...... What are you doing! Put me down!" You slapped your palm until it was red, but he showed no particular reaction. You spoke with a sobbing voice, while he kept his eyes straight ahead, speaking in a hoarse, somewhat cold tone: "Why should I listen to you?" ”

You're stuck. Indeed, he doesn't need to listen to you—after all, you have nothing to do with him. Still, your face flushed: "But...... But you can't do that! I don't like it this way! "Actually, you're feeling guilty. You don't even know if what you say will actually work—it hasn't been like this before. Most of the time, it's only after you faint from crying that he comes to find you. By the time you wake up, you'll already be lying in the lounge under a blanket. If you ask him, he'll say it was pippins who covered you up.

But he didn't answer you because he walked quickly. By now, he was back in the lounge, decisively sat down on the sofa, then sat across from you holding the TV's head. His screen went pitch black again, and then another scene flashed before appearing. It was a puzzle game you bought some time ago but never had time to play. You've always loved this series of puzzle games, and now that the sequel came out, you naturally bought it.

You glare at the unresponsive Tenna, suspecting he's doing it on purpose, but you still pick up the controller and start controlling the detective character on the screen—better not to go against the game.

Emotions come and go quickly. You quickly become fully immersed in the game. However, this sequel is actually a combination of open world exploration and puzzles. You're a directionally challenged person who searched for a long time in the game before finally finding your way. Anton Tenna kept watching your movements, and finally, unable to resist, the TV started playing again: "You don't look very smart, just spinning in place. Aren't you afraid of getting dizzy?" ”

“…… None of your business! How well I play the game has nothing to do with you. "You grumbled and took a detour for more than ten minutes before finally finding the right direction.

"It matters. I can't stand seeing such a dumb detective on my TV screen." He said. Alright, even though how you want to play the game has nothing to do with him, he's happy to watch you sit here a little longer—just a little longer, to focus intently on him. But since you've been lost all along, you open your phone to start looking up guides, figuring out the right route, while Tenna suddenly feels bored.

It took a lot of effort to calm down—or rather, you know that's just how he is. What just happened was a silent reconciliation between you two. But now you threw the controller aside, stood up angrily, and shouted, "You can't stand it! That other TV always works! I need to find a TV that won't disturb me while I'm gaming!" Saying that, you walked past him and tried to walk outside. Yes, I don't know why he threw you on the sofa opposite the lounge door, and you sat at the doorway. The next second, Tenna's big hand grabbed the back of your neck and easily lifted you up.

You frown, swinging your arms and legs back and forth, trying to shake yourself off, but Tenna's hand holds you firmly, and no matter what, you can't reach the ground.

"What do you want! I already said I hate you...... You want me to stay here—don't you feel bad yourself?" You felt like crying again, sniffled, but tried to stay calm as you spoke.

Tenna's screen went black again, he turned his head, and you felt he was watching you.

His voice was a bit stiff: "In the world of television, besides me, there's no other television." ”

Then he put you down.

You were incredulous, turned your head to look at him: "Why? Isn't this the TV world?" ”

"This is a dark world built from your home." His voice was flat, without any ups and downs. You paused, then thought about it and realized it was true—you had bought this somewhat damaged TV when collecting scrap before. Although you didn't watch much shows, you still had many game discs left unfinished. Later on, inexplicably, a dark world appeared.

"Oh...... Then I'll go back to the Light World to play games. At least you won't be able to talk." You muttered a few words, then prepared to find the exit from the Dark World. Honestly, playing games in the Light World is more fun. In the Dark World, you feel like you're just being the puncher every day.

Tenna didn't react, just moved a bit and blocked the lounge door: "You can't go back to the world of light." "If you return to the world of light—then what does he even think he is?" And he didn't want you to find out that he'd already had his staff lock the doors to the dark fountain and the main entrance. For now, you can't go back.

"You said if you won't let me go back, I can't leave...... Step aside!" As you spoke, you tried to pull Tenna's heavy mechanical body away, but unfortunately, you couldn't. You didn't even shake him in the slightest. You're so angry you want to kick him twice, but you're afraid you'll hurt your own foot.

His screen lit up. After a brief channel switch, the TV screen froze back on the game screen. This time, the game map opened without receiving your instructions and marked the correct destination markers. He had just checked the game channels that were still available. Fortunately, there were indeed some guides for this game, so he marked the guide as provided.

Your movements pause for a moment, then you look up hesitantly at the TV screen. To be honest, you don't understand why Tenna is doing this, but he just grabs the controller you just dropped and puts it back into your hand. Instinctively, you press the joystick as the detective character runs forward, gradually approaching the destination.

You pouted. If that's the case, then you could keep playing...... But you felt a bit awkward: "...... Fine, I want yogurt, but you're blocking the door, I can't get out." You started awkwardly changing the subject. Although you did pull the door away, Tenna was big enough to sit here and blocked the entrance, not letting you out.

Tenna didn't move, just pointed to the small door beside her: "There's a small door in the lounge. You can go get it yourself." ”

“…… Is it good? ”

"I don't know, I haven't tried it before."

"How long have you been sitting there?" You walked through that small door, and as soon as you opened it, you sneezed a few times. You never expected that the kitchen was covered in dust.

This question made Tenna hesitate for a moment: "...... About three or four years ago?" "Never replaced them...... Ah, a few years ago, they did replace a batch when he first arrived at this house. Back then, you would still excitedly hold the TV and say you liked it the most.

But later, you never said those things again.

You stuck out your tongue and closed the small door: "I don't want ...... It's been spoiled for ages, hasn't it? I want you to get me fresh yogurt! Preferably with a lower viscosity, I'm thirsty!" You started pointing around fiercely.

Then Tenna took out a cup of warm water and handed it to you: "Then you should drink more hot water." "After all, you feel thirsty.

You choked, then glared at him again: "No, I want yogurt, go get it!" "Actually, you just wanted to sneak out of the lounge while telling him to do something...... Somehow, ever since he took you back to the lounge, your sixth sense has been alerting you.

TBC.

You glared angrily at Tenna sitting there, then banged his TV screen twice.

“…… Let me out!" "You don't want to make any more excuses, so just be honest. Anyway, you're not in the mood to play games right now...... Even if he's already marked the path for you, you still don't want to keep playing.

Tenna really did turn back into a pitch-black screen. He stood up, looking down at you, and you instinctively took two steps back, thinking you really didn't need to be afraid—after all, control of this TV was still in your hands! So you pouted and looked at him with your head held high.

"Then I'll go with you." His voice was hoarse, a bit cold, and deliberate.

You can't help but feel a bit scared, since such a big TV is following behind you, but soon, you can't care about those things anymore. You run to the kitchen not far away and grab a box of fresh yogurt, while Tenna follows behind you at a leisurely pace. His footsteps are especially clear, making it impossible for you to run quickly or shake him off, because he always manages to keep up with you.

You slurped your yogurt and tried several twists and turns, finally reaching the entrance where you once came from the world of light. Although the door was tightly shut, you still happily stepped forward and pulled the doorknob—the door was locked and wouldn't open.

You pause for a moment, and Tenna steps forward, standing in front of you to block the door.

“…… No, you can't go back." He thought for a moment, but finally spoke so firmly. To be honest, Tenna had thought about quite a lot. At this moment, he should have begged the Lightfolk to keep watching him, to beg the Lightfolk to stay here and immerse themselves in the fun of the game. He could no longer tolerate losing—but he didn't want to do it either, so all he could do now was look down at you and say those cold, cold words.

You glared at him angrily: "Why should I! Why do you make decisions for me on your own...... I—when I get back, I'm going to replace your broken TV!" This statement sounded a bit uncertain, since you didn't know when you'd finally return to the world of light, to go home—even though you were the only one at home, that was a place where all your sense of security existed, a place where you could feel at ease. As for family...... You no longer had that kind of thing, ever since they wanted to abandon you for your brother.

Tenna stepped forward, closing in on you two steps. You instinctively swallowed a couple of mouthfuls, feeling a bit guilty, and stepped back two paces, but still looked at him with a stubborn attitude.

His voice seemed a bit urgent—maybe it was just your imagination, your fantasy, since you were nervous right now.

“…… Change the TV? Do other TVs really know your preferences so well? He finished speaking rapidly, then fell silent again. You don't know why the screen stayed black, or why those two antennas seemed to have just dropped for a moment before quickly rising again.

"Anyway, you can gradually learn about your preferences. I usually play those games often, so I'll know if I switch to another TV." You muttered softly, your voice growing quieter and quieter, because for some reason, he was bent over. The pitch-black screen leaned close to you, and you couldn't help but feel scared.

"Do other TV shows understand your hand fetish?" For some reason, you find his voice a bit eerie, eeri.

"So what...... If you lose, you'll just start over again. At least you won't be like you, saying a few words to me just because you can't beat me!" You glared at him twice, then covered your mouth. You know you've been speaking quite boldly lately—after all, this scary-looking TV wasn't trying to stop you.

"You only want a silent TV?" For some reason, after saying this, he fell silent as well.

"Right...... That's right! I want to play the game properly, I don't want to be disturbed—oh, even if it's a TV person! "You muster up the courage and shout loudly, closing your eyes.

“…… You don't need to return to the world of light. "After the unsettling silence, you only hear these words, then you feel yourself being lifted up, and when you open your eyes to look, you find yourself sitting on Tenna's arm.

"Put me down! It's not like I can't walk on my own!" You tried hard to kick your legs twice, but it didn't help, while Tenna just talked to herself.

"If you go back, you'll be alone too. Why not stay here...... Don't you think that's great?" It sounded like a question, but he just spoke in Chen Dataset's tone, very certain. Probably because he's certain you'll stay here.

"So what...... I'm doing fine on my own. "You muttered quietly, not knowing why he was walking so fast, but once again returned to the lounge and put you down on the sofa. He handed you the game controller, then sat at the door again, propping up his head on the TV and reopening the game screen.

You threw that box of yogurt on the ground during your struggle. You felt a bit regretful you hadn't finished it, so you smacked your lips, propped your chin up and boredly controlled your game character to reach your destination. After pondering for a while, you finally deduced the truth behind the incident and successfully solved the case.

You set the next side quest as a tracking item, and immediately a navigation path appeared before your eyes. Only then did you realize that Tenna had already marked the destination markers on the map. You cleared your throat twice, wanting to thank him, but...... He was so quiet it was almost eerie, making you hesitant to speak.

Although it was a bit bumpy, you eventually reached your destination. This case was relatively simple, you solved it quickly, and then it seemed to trigger the main quest—well, the marked points still appeared on the map.

I have to say, even though you're bored and playing games, time still flies by—it's just that you're not used to it. After all, Tenna has been quiet for so long that you can't help but ask, "Why did you suddenly stop talking?" ”

"This is your request." For some reason, you somehow sensed a hint of sarcasm in that sentence.

“…… Anyway, thank you." You cleared your throat twice and touched your nose. Although it was indeed your request, suddenly losing your voice made you feel a bit lonely.

After all, in this dark world, no one plays with you. Except for this TV—only this one. The other Dark People somehow run away as soon as they see you.

"Oh." He answered you very quickly in a short voice.

You tapped your controller; the game screen was still loading. Next up was a cutscene CG, but you didn't want to watch. You hesitated for a moment and asked, "But in the dark world, I'm also alone......"

Just being honest, or maybe you're just making things up.

You hear Tenna snort coldly, half mocking: "Are you ignoring something that exists?" ”

“…… Yes, yes, you are here, but days like this are no different from when I am in the Light World. "Because there's nothing to do, you play games every day in the Light World. Anyway...... Let's talk about the future later. When you were in the Light World, you were clearly facing this TV every day too.

Oh, you just said in a fit of anger that you want to replace this TV when you get back.

"Oh, so what are you talking to me now?" His voice returned to a cold, rigid tone, and you started to doubt yourself, because you strangely felt he seemed angry.

“…… Didn't say anything. I just found it a bit strange that you hadn't spoken for a long time. "You whispered, watching as the main storyline animation finally finished, you began to control the character to move.

But Tenna suddenly closed the game screen, and what appeared immediately was that face that had never been happy, with a downward curve at the corner of her mouth.

"Because it's bedtime now." He said as he stepped forward, took the game console from your hand, and then pushed you into a room. This room had been your residence recently—very simple, with no decorations, except for a poster of Anton Tenna stuck on the wall.

But today, when you lay down on the bed, for some reason you found a TV plushie on the bed—just a black screen, with a bent wire wrapped in tape, exactly like the TV in your Light World. Looking around, you realized the room had become exactly the same as your bedroom in the Light World.

…… When did he do it? He's been with you all along...... Could it be when you ran out of anger earlier? You don't know, you just hugged the stuffed animal in your arms tightly—by the way, if you sleep holding the TV at night, would it be radiated? But originally, it was just a furry stuffed animal, how could that be? Unless you have a plush allergy.

Thinking this, you can't help but yawn, rub your nose, and quickly crawl under the covers. Alright then...... Better get a good sleep first.

You closed your eyes, but couldn't help but smile. Indeed, perhaps the world of the dark would be warmer, since it felt more like home, much better than the empty houses in the world of light.

But you can't give up! After all, staying in the dark world all the time is nothing!

In a daze, you drift off to sleep—in your dream, you're sitting on the sofa playing a game, seemingly in the dark world. You must be playing some game that requires skill. Tenna is still holding his head on the TV, seemingly mocking you a bit. You start arguing with him, get so angry you try to run out through the lounge door.

He wrapped his arms around the waist and lifted you up, the screen pitch black, and said slowly, "I know you love me." ”

"But you know—"

Before you could finish the last half sentence, you woke up from the dream, gasping for breath, thinking how he could possibly know what you were thinking. Uneasy, you turned over and fell back asleep again.

When you fully wake up, you rub your eyes and stared blankly at the TV plush toy tightly hugged in your arms. You don't even know what you're thinking, anyway...... You're probably just daydreaming. Are you wondering if Tenna specially arranged this room? After all...... Other than him, the other Dark People in this TV world probably aren't so familiar with your room's layout.

…… After all, you only occasionally feel a bit weak and empty because the house is empty and empty, then drag the TV into your room, then sit on the bed hugging your knees, just watching the show absentmindedly, or rather, you just need a little sound to dispel your fear and loneliness when you're alone.

You get dressed and eat the steaming hot breakfast on the table, then walk out of the room. For some reason, Tenna is already sitting on a chair in the lounge. His screen is still pitch black, looking like he's resting with his eyes closed.

You hesitated for a moment. To be honest, you hadn't figured out what you should do today, nor had you figured it out...... What kind of attitude should you have when facing this guy? After all, although what he said was really harsh, the room was decorated and there were other aspects...... You still felt a bit grateful.

But you don't know if he'll actually agree to your invitation. After all...... What if you're just delusional? What if you just assume he likes you? That's a huge misunderstanding that absolutely must not happen—

So you sat on the sofa opposite him, hesitated for a moment, then cleared your throat twice and said, "You...... Want to play games with me? ”

His screen lit up. He seemed to be looking up at you, or maybe just looking down at you with a half-smile: "Oh?" Didn't you think my words were disturbing you? ”

You always feel like Tenna is being sarcastic...... Hmm, maybe this isn't just your imagination.

When he brings up this topic, you feel a bit awkward too, lowering your head and not daring to look at that TV again. You just think about it and feel that giving him a chance to play with him is a win—others might not even get the chance to be your playmate! Of course, that's just because you don't have friends.

So you put your hands on your hips, lifted your head, and deliberately spoke with a high vantage point: "Hmph...... This is an opportunity that others can't even dream of. Why don't you cherish it?" After saying that, you swallowed hard; you really couldn't get used to this way of speaking.

So you sneak a glance at Tenna out of the corner of your eye, only to see his screen go completely black again. He just coldly responds with an 'oh' and then ignores you.

"What do you want to do today?" He just casually switched to the next topic.

You thought he agreed, and excitedly ordered a two-player cooperative puzzle game. Instead, you watched Tenna hold his TV head, projecting the game's image onto the screen.

“…… This is a two-player game. You can't just let me play with two controllers by myself, can I?" You pointed at yourself in disbelief.

"Didn't you say playing with you was a chance that no one could even dream of? Just find someone else to play with." Tenna lowered her voice. For some reason, you always felt he was a bit sarcastic...... But you couldn't see anything from this screen right now.

If they go out to find other Dark People...... Most of them probably wouldn't dare come over, since most are still afraid of Tenna, the big boss.

Of course, not long after you left the house, you felt that burning gaze again. You sneaked a glance out of the corner of your eye, and sure enough, Tenna had followed, though his hiding skills weren't very good...... In any case, you just sighed silently in your heart and ignored him, just staring at the rings displayed on the counter and began to wonder what style you should choose.

Maybe because you had been inside the jewelry store for quite a while, and you chatted with the clerk a bit, you only felt that burning, sticky gaze growing even more damp, or rather, it seemed to carry a hint of darkness. You sighed helplessly, greeted the clerk, then crossed your arms and walked out of the store, speaking leisurely, "Alright, Tenna, come out. I know you're nearby." ”

But after you said this, there was no reaction from those around you; even that burning gaze vanished for a moment before returning to you.

You sighed softly in your heart, then deliberately raised your voice: "Oh no, it looks like my boyfriend got lost...... Where did he go? I can't go without my boyfriend. Maybe I should just find someone else, like the ...... in the store." As you spoke, you looked toward the jewelry store, about to walk inside, when you felt your arm being grabbed tightly—it was Tenna. Once again, he showed that gritted teeth expression. You instinctively shivered, for some reason feeling that his expression really carried a hint of horror and bloodiness.

“…… I'm your boyfriend. Who else do you want to find?" After saying this, he turned off the screen and turned his head away. He didn't look very happy, or rather, a bit embarrassed, but at least his voice was extremely rapid.

You blink at him, tilting your head slightly: "I'm not looking for anyone else, I'm just looking for you......" You want to add one more sentence, but Tenna suddenly steps forward and hugs you. You pause, only hearing his low, cold voice, sounding a bit listless: "Don't say things like that again next time." Also...... "Don't leave me alone and leave ......"

His voice grew quieter and softer, sounding stammering, and you could only helplessly hug him back, then cupped his head and gently kissed him on the screen.

"Alright, next time we go out, I'll call you to join me...... Actually, I just wanted to prepare a surprise for you. "You feel a bit helpless, but Tenna still holds you tightly and refuses to let go.

You have no choice but to soften your tone and lower your voice: "Do you want to see what surprise it is?" ”

Tenna didn't answer you; all you could see was him nod. You took a small box out of your pocket, opened it, and inside were two rings, each engraved with your name. The conversation you had just had with the jewelry store staff was about choosing a ring that fit Tenna's size.

"Ta-da...... Hmm, cough cough...... If you don't like it, I'll put it away...... Seeing Tenna showing no reaction, you rubbed the tip of your nose and felt a bit guilty, wondering if you were a bit too impatient...... You know that even though Tenna got the answer from you, he still wasn't very calm. Otherwise, why would he keep asking you to repeat that sentence?

Tenna was stunned for a moment, shook his head, and simply reached out his hand toward you. You took out a larger ring engraved with your name and slipped it onto his ring finger, and Tenna eagerly slipped the ring with his name onto your left ring finger.

You saw his screen light up again, and this time the antenna just drooped, gently shaking. You sighed, grabbed Tenna's hand, and softly said, "Alright, satisfied now, right? Let's go home." ”

Tenna just muttered, and once again, fresh flowers sprouted from his nose, some drifting and falling to the ground, while the corners of his lips couldn't help but curl up, revealing a smile that could be described as silly. He just whispered softly, as if his lips were off the mark: "Mm, Y/N, I love you." Then he returned and held your hand tightly. You were a bit troubled by his movement, but you still let him hold your hand, just responding casually.

"Yeah, I love you too...... Alright, alright, let's go home...... Actually, from the very beginning, the warmth on your face hasn't faded. If you didn't think love means you should give the other person something better, you wouldn't have done this. You don't know what love really looks like, you haven't experienced it, but maybe this sweet feeling now is love...... Maybe that's all there is to it.

You don't even know what love should do, it's just ...... Since Tenna wants you to be with him forever, then just stay with him forever; since Tenna wants you to love him, and you say you love him, then just say it out loud...... Maybe all these things are because you love him.

You don't know, you just know that at times like this, you yourself are happy...... That's all, because you also long for his company, for his love. Although perhaps neither of you truly knows what love should look like, just as it is now, it's good.

So you naturally reminded him, "Mm...... Then come with me to the shooting range this afternoon, and watch a movie with me tonight. What do you want to see?" You gave up on the order, but...... When you call out 'tenna' like this, you're still a bit unaccustomed. After all, you've used to calling him to keep you company with orders.

"Well, then...... Watch "Heartbeat." "Tenna said, you know, this is a famous romance film.

And you just smiled and nodded: "Alright, let's watch this tonight...... Oh, and um, you want to sleep with me." You said this, shyly pinching your fingers. Tenna just nodded gently, his dark screen turned on, and he smiled at you.

Tenna was sitting on the sofa at that moment, and you sat down and gently intertwined your fingers with Tenna. Tenna just casually said, "What else do you want to do?" ”

"I'm going to the bookstore! There was a book last time I had been eyeing for a long time, but it was on the shelf, and I couldn't reach it at all...... Tenna, go and help me get it. ”

"Alright."

You squinted and smiled, then moved a little closer, then leaned against Tenna's shoulder.

In this gamble, no one is a complete winner, nor is there a complete loser, after all...... This is just the story of two gamblers who bet on themselves and ultimately win against each other.
